00:16:35.208 The Newbie Help Room
00:16:35.208 -=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=
00:16:35.208 Welcome to TorilMUD!  You can start your adventures here in the following
00:16:35.209 three ways:

00:16:35.210 Type ENTER TORIL to enter your guild and begin your life in the
00:16:35.211 hometown that you have chosen.  If you are familiar with the areas and
00:16:35.211 gameplay of our mud, this will be the most familiar to you.  At any point
00:16:35.212 during your first 20 levels you can type "RECALL" and be brought back
00:16:35.214 to this room if you feel you need help.

00:16:35.214 Each of the exits leading from this room will bring you to various guilds
00:16:35.215 within the city of Scardale; a city created specifically for new players.
00:16:35.216 Scardale is filled with mobs to help teach you about the mud, and help
00:16:35.217 tutor you on the basics of each of the classes. There are also several
00:16:35.218 zones that were created for exploring and experiencing for when you are
00:16:35.220 ready to leave the city and venture into the country side.

00:16:35.220 To begin, type "equipment" to check out your gear, 'put all bag' to place
00:16:35.221 the rest of your supplies into your bag, and then 'get book bag' to be able to
00:16:35.223 examine a very helpful guide. Type read book to view the first page of the book.

00:16:35.224 Type WEST to enter the guild for warriors, paladins, blackguards,
00:16:35.224 rangers and hexblades. Type EAST to enter the guild for mages;
00:16:35.225 enchanters, invokers, illusionist, elementalist and necromancers. Type
00:16:35.226 NORTH to enter the guild for clerics, shamans and druids. Type SOUTH
00:16:35.227 to enter the guild for rogues, bards and battlechanters. Type DOWN to
00:16:35.228 enter the guild for psionicists.

00:16:35.229 If you're completely overwhelmed and have no idea what you should do or
00:16:35.230 where you should start, simply type "nhc Help, I'm confused!", and
00:16:35.231 one of the other players will answer your pleas (if there's one online
00:16:35.232 at the time).  At any time you can also peruse our comprehensive help and
00:16:35.234 informational files.  Type HELP or INFO for further instructions on these
00:16:35.235 files.

00:16:35.235 Hopefully the help files available to you at any time, the newbie
00:16:35.236 experience generating zone, and the tutorial will ease your transition from
00:16:35.237 the dull and mundane Real World into the fantastic and addictive world of
00:16:35.238 TorilMUD.  Enjoy!
00:16:35.238 -=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=

00:29:44.024 of the city.  The fountain was carved from a single piece 
00:29:44.025 of granite, and was formed into the shape of four dolphins
00:29:44.026 jumping from the water and soaring into the heavens.  Water

00:41:20.176                

00:41:20.181 The Newbie Help Room
00:41:20.181 -=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=
00:41:20.182 Welcome to TorilMUD!  You can start your adventures here in the following
00:41:20.183 three ways:

00:41:20.183 Type ENTER TORIL to enter your guild and begin your life in the
00:41:20.184 hometown that you have chosen.  If you are familiar with the areas and
00:41:20.185 gameplay of our mud, this will be the most familiar to you.  At any point
00:41:20.185 during your first 20 levels you can type "RECALL" and be brought back
00:41:20.186 to this room if you feel you need help.

00:41:20.187 Each of the exits leading from this room will bring you to various guilds
00:41:20.188 within the city of Scardale; a city created specifically for new players.
00:41:20.189 Scardale is filled with mobs to help teach you about the mud, and help
00:41:20.190 tutor you on the basics of each of the classes. There are also several
00:41:20.191 zones that were created for exploring and experiencing for when you are
00:41:20.192 ready to leave the city and venture into the country side.

00:41:20.193 To begin, type "equipment" to check out your gear, 'put all bag' to place
00:41:20.195 the rest of your supplies into your bag, and then 'get book bag' to be able to
00:41:20.196 examine a very helpful guide. Type read book to view the first page of the book.

00:41:20.197 Type WEST to enter the guild for warriors, paladins, blackguards,
00:41:20.198 rangers and hexblades. Type EAST to enter the guild for mages;
00:41:20.199 enchanters, invokers, illusionist, elementalist and necromancers. Type
00:41:20.200 NORTH to enter the guild for clerics, shamans and druids. Type SOUTH
00:41:20.201 to enter the guild for rogues, bards and battlechanters. Type DOWN to
00:41:20.202 enter the guild for psionicists.

00:41:20.202 If you're completely overwhelmed and have no idea what you should do or
00:41:20.204 where you should start, simply type "nhc Help, I'm confused!", and
00:41:20.204 one of the other players will answer your pleas (if there's one online
00:41:20.205 at the time).  At any time you can also peruse our comprehensive help and
00:41:20.206 informational files.  Type HELP or INFO for further instructions on these
00:41:20.207 files.

00:41:20.207 Hopefully the help files available to you at any time, the newbie
00:41:20.208 experience generating zone, and the tutorial will ease your transition from
00:41:20.209 the dull and mundane Real World into the fantastic and addictive world of
00:41:20.211 TorilMUD.  Enjoy!
00:41:20.211 -=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=

00:42:02.827   A beautiful stone fountain sits here in the exact center

01:34:55.675 A Blood Stained Hallway
01:34:55.676   No illumination whatsoever exists here, as the priests are 
01:34:55.677 more comfortable in utter darkness.  Five or six yards up on 
01:34:55.677 the east and west walls are set high stained glass windows 
01:34:55.678 with motifs from the mythology of Bane and Helm. The walls 
01:34:55.679 are painted jet black with a splattering of dried blood; 
01:34:55.680 inscribed with flowing spidery runes, which stretch all 
01:34:55.680 across the hall's perimeter.  The floors were once a white 
01:34:55.681 smooth stone, but are now nothing more then cracked and 
01:34:55.682 uneven pieces of rubble.  The entire hall was constructed 
01:34:55.683 of well polished oak cut from the tallest trees from the 
01:34:55.683 near by forest.  Most of the wood is soot stained and smeared 
01:34:55.684 with blood.  The hallway runs west, circling the western
01:34:55.684  portion of the temple.  A large wooden door to the east
01:34:55.685 leads into the main sanctuary of the temple, while the one
01:34:55.686 to the south leads to a small chamber.

01:35:59.817 The Newbie Help Room
01:35:59.817 -=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=
01:35:59.818 Welcome to TorilMUD!  You can start your adventures here in the following
01:35:59.819 three ways:

01:35:59.819 Type ENTER TORIL to enter your guild and begin your life in the
01:35:59.820 hometown that you have chosen.  If you are familiar with the areas and
01:35:59.821 gameplay of our mud, this will be the most familiar to you.  At any point
01:35:59.822 during your first 20 levels you can type "RECALL" and be brought back
01:35:59.822 to this room if you feel you need help.

01:35:59.824 Each of the exits leading from this room will bring you to various guilds
01:35:59.825 within the city of Scardale; a city created specifically for new players.
01:35:59.826 Scardale is filled with mobs to help teach you about the mud, and help
01:35:59.827 tutor you on the basics of each of the classes. There are also several
01:35:59.828 zones that were created for exploring and experiencing for when you are
01:35:59.829 ready to leave the city and venture into the country side.

01:35:59.830 To begin, type "equipment" to check out your gear, 'put all bag' to place
01:35:59.831 the rest of your supplies into your bag, and then 'get book bag' to be able to
01:35:59.832 examine a very helpful guide. Type read book to view the first page of the book.

01:35:59.834 Type WEST to enter the guild for warriors, paladins, blackguards,
01:35:59.834 rangers and hexblades. Type EAST to enter the guild for mages;
01:35:59.835 enchanters, invokers, illusionist, elementalist and necromancers. Type
01:35:59.836 NORTH to enter the guild for clerics, shamans and druids. Type SOUTH
01:35:59.837 to enter the guild for rogues, bards and battlechanters. Type DOWN to
01:35:59.838 enter the guild for psionicists.

01:35:59.838 If you're completely overwhelmed and have no idea what you should do or
01:35:59.839 where you should start, simply type "nhc Help, I'm confused!", and
01:35:59.840 one of the other players will answer your pleas (if there's one online
01:35:59.841 at the time).  At any time you can also peruse our comprehensive help and
01:35:59.842 informational files.  Type HELP or INFO for further instructions on these
01:35:59.844 files.

01:35:59.844 Hopefully the help files available to you at any time, the newbie
01:35:59.845 experience generating zone, and the tutorial will ease your transition from
01:35:59.846 the dull and mundane Real World into the fantastic and addictive world of
01:35:59.847 TorilMUD.  Enjoy!
01:35:59.847 -=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=-=
